Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in electrochemistry theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 587 and the invariant I(A,B) = 348 mod 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the electrochemistry space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 587 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 348. QED.
